=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
There are N buildings, building 1, building 2, \ldots, building N, arranged in this order in a straight line from west to east. Building 1 is the westernmost, and building N is the easternmost. The height of building i\ (1\leq i\leq N) is H_i.
For a pair of integers (i,j)\ (1\leq i\lt j\leq N), building j can be seen from building i if the following condition is satisfied.

- There is no building taller than building j between buildings i and j. In other words, there is no integer k\ (i\lt k\lt j) such that H_k > H_j.

You are given Q queries. In the i-th query, given a pair of integers (l_i,r_i)\ (l_i\lt r_i), find the number of buildings to the east of building r_i (that is, buildings r_i + 1, r_i + 2, \ldots, N) that can be seen from both buildings l_i and r_i.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N Q
H_1 H_2 \ldots H_N
l_1 r_1
l_2 r_2
\vdots
l_Q r_Q

Output

Print Q lines. The i-th line (1 \leq i \leq Q) should contain the answer to the i-th query.

Constraints


- 2 \leq N \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq Q \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq H_i \leq N
- H_i\neq H_j\ (i\neq j)
- 1 \leq l_i < r_i \leq N
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5 3
2 1 4 3 5
1 2
3 5
1 4

Sample Output 1

2
0
1


- For the first query, among the buildings to the east of building 2, buildings 3 and 5 can be seen from both buildings 1 and 2, so the answer is 2.
- For the second query, there are no buildings to the east of building 5.
- For the third query, among the buildings to the east of building 4, building 5 can be seen from both buildings 1 and 4, so the answer is 1.

Sample Input 2

10 10
2 1 5 3 4 6 9 8 7 10
3 9
2 5
4 8
5 6
3 8
2 10
7 8
6 7
8 10
4 10

Sample Output 2

1
3
1
2
1
0
1
1
0
0

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Implementation:
import sys
def main():
    data=list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    it=iter(data)
    N=next(it); Q=next(it)
    H=[0]+[next(it) for _ in range(N)]
    p=[0]*(N+1)
    st=[]
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        hi=H[i]
        while st and H[st[-1]] < hi:
            st.pop()
        p[i]=st[-1] if st else 0
        st.append(i)
    buckets=[[] for _ in range(N+1)]  # p can N-1; use N+1
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        buckets[p[i]].append(i)
    queries=[]
    for qi in range(Q):
        l=next(it); r=next(it)
        queries.append((l,r,qi))
    queries.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    bit=[0]*(N+1)
    def add(idx, val):
        while idx<=N:
            bit[idx]+=val
            idx += idx & -idx
    def sum_prefix(idx):
        s=0
        while idx>0:
            s+=bit[idx]
            idx -= idx & -idx
        return s
    ans=[0]*Q
    cur=-1
    for l,r,qi in queries:
        while cur < l:
            cur += 1
            # cur maybe N? l≤N-1, but p can N? no. bucket length N+1, cur max N-1. okay.
            for x in buckets[cur]:
                add(x,1)
        total=cur? Wait after while cur==l. Need total number added = sum_prefix(N) maybe. But can maintain added_count to avoid prefix(N). But prefix(N) O(logN) okay. Suffix = total_added - sum_prefix(r). We can maintain added_count += len(buckets[cur]) when adding. Then ans = added_count - sum_prefix(r). Since all added have p≤l; positions ≤r counted by prefix. Good.
    Need added_count variable.
But if queries sorted by l, cur may skip values; while cur<l adds all buckets from cur+1 to l. If l can be 0? constraints l>=1. cur=-1. okay.
Need be careful if l=N? l<r≤N so l≤N-1. cur max N-1. But if l=N? no. Buckets length N+1 safe.
Then output '\n'.join(map(str,ans)).

Let's ensure code style:
import sys

def main():
    input_data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not input_data:
        return
    idx = 0
    N = input_data[idx]; Q = input_data[idx+1]; idx += 2
    H = [0] + input_data[idx:idx+N]; idx += N
    p = [0]*(N+1)
    stack = []
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        hi = H[i]
        while stack and H[stack[-1]] <= hi:
            stack.pop()
        p[i] = stack[-1] if stack else 0
        stack.append(i)
    buckets = [[] for _ in range(N+1)]
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        buckets[p[i]].append(i)
    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        l = input_data[idx]; r = input_data[idx+1]; idx += 2
        queries.append((l, r, qi))
    queries.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    bit = [0]*(N+1)
    def add(i, v):
        while i <= N:
            bit[i] += v
            i += i & -i
    def pref(i):
        s = 0
        while i > 0:
            s += bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return s
    ans = [0]*Q
    cur = -1
    added = 0
    for l, r, qi in queries:
        while cur < l:
            cur += 1
            for x in buckets[cur]:
                add(x, 1)
                added += 1
        ans[qi] = added - pref(r)
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Now, let's decide final code with readline:
```
import sys

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    H = [0] + list(map(int, input().split()))
    p = [0]*(N+1)
    st = []
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        hi = H[i]
        while st and H[st[-1]] <= hi:
            st.pop()
        p[i] = st[-1] if st else 0
        st.append(i)
    buckets = [[] for _ in range(N+1)]
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        buckets[p[i]].append(i)
    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        l, r = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((l, r, qi))
    queries.sort()
    bit = [0]*(N+1)
    def add(i, v):
        while i <= N:
            bit[i] += v
            i += i & -i
    def sum_prefix(i):
        s = 0
        while i > 0:
            s += bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return s
    ans = [0]*Q
    cur = -1
    added = 0
    for l, r, qi in queries:
        while cur < l:
            cur += 1
            for x in buckets[cur]:
                add(x, 1)
                added += 1
        ans[qi] = added - sum_prefix(r)
    print('\n'.join(map(str, ans)))
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's write final code:

```
import sys

def main():
    it = iter(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = next(it)
    Q = next(it)
    H = [0] + [next(it) for _ in range(N)]

    prev_greater = [0] * (N + 1)
    stack = []
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        hi = H[i]
        while stack and H[stack[-1]] <= hi:
            stack.pop()
        prev_greater[i] = stack[-1] if stack else 0
        stack.append(i)

    buckets = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        buckets[prev_greater[i]].append(i)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        l = next(it)
        r = next(it)
        queries.append((l, r, qi))
    del it

    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (N + 1)

    def add(idx, val):
        while idx <= N:
            bit[idx] += val
            idx += idx & -idx

    def prefix_sum(idx):
        s = 0
        while idx > 0:
            s += bit[idx]
            idx -= idx & -idx
        return s

    ans = [0] * Q
    cur = -1
    added = 0

    for l, r, qi in queries:
        while cur < l:
            cur += 1
            for x in buckets[cur]:
                add(x, 1)
                added += 1
        ans[qi] = added - prefix_sum(r)

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
